Turkey's geography, terrorism, and the tragedy it causes are the closest and the closer it feels. As a responsible member of the international community, Turkey is struggling with this severe test, while at the same time making intense efforts to address the negative consequences of terrorism on regional and global scale. Terrorism is one of the biggest obstacles to global peace and stability. As Turkey, we've been living very closely for years, and we've felt it up to our bones, and we know that it will make a country and humanity pay for what it's worth. In order for the fight against terrorism to succeed, internationally sincere cooperation and solidarity are essential. Turkey has done its part so far in the context of international co-operation in the fight against terrorism, it will continue to do so after that. With this determination, Turkey has been co-chairing with the United States since the establishment of the Anti-Terror Global Forum. In addition, Turkey was one of the first countries to declare the DAEŞ terrorist organisation as a terrorist organisation, with the decision of the Council of Ministers of September 30, 2013. One of the most important principles in counterterrorism is no doubt to fight terror organizations without discrimination. Segregation among terrorist organisations would do the most harm to combat this global problem. With that understanding, we continue and continue to fight DAS, al-Qaeda and other terrorist organizations, just as we fight PKK, NYD, YPG and DHKP-C. Turkey has also expressed in international platforms that it expects the same attitude and the same determination from all countries, the fight against terrorism is not only Turkey's problem, but the problem of all humanity. At this point, it is also necessary to be clear that my terrorist is good, your terrorist is evil. It is wrong to act with reason. Terror is terror, and those who kill innocent lives are terrorists. A terrorist and a terrorist organization are no good. For example, it would be unacceptable for a PYD terrorist organization to be seen as a lovely terrorist organization because it's fighting against DAEŞ. It has nothing to do with the fact that the PKK or any other terrorist organization is representing our Kurdish citizens or fighting for their rights, since terrorism has no religion, nationality, or race. tbmm-2016-03-05sit01spe0009par0406	Mr. President, I have made a pledge on behalf of my group regarding the budget for the Ministry of Environment and Urbanization, as part of the 2016 Central Management Budget Bill. Before I begin, I salute the General Assembly. According to UN readings, the world's population was estimated to reach nine billion by 2050. The rapid population growth, industrialisation, distorted urbanization, the rapid consumption of regional wars and natural resources is causing environmental problems to increase. The solution to global environmental problems that threaten the world we live in requires international cooperation and active participation at a bilateral, regional and multilateral level as well as national effort. To leave a better world for future generations, it is necessary to prevent pollution, protect nature, protect green areas, and enhance them. Like all over the world, the environment is becoming increasingly important and the work is being done to reduce environmental problems. To this end, environmental protection, prevention of pollution within the framework of relevant regulations, particularly the Constitution and the Environmental Law, and the principle of sustainable development. Our government considers the environment, the water, the air, the forest, and the land as one, and conducts its work in fullness. Rapid population growth, migration, industrialization and exhaust emissions are among the main causes of air pollution. Air quality monitoring stations have been set up in 81 cities all over Turkey to monitor its air quality. By means of these stations, daily, hourly, and momentary measurements are being taken, and measures are being taken accordingly. As a result of such investments, the number of cities with natural gas rose from 9 to 77 in 2002 as of today, while the remaining four cities continue to transport natural gas. This is an indication of the importance of air quality. Dear lawmakers, waste management -- the main problem of many countries around the world -- is also the head of careful work in our country. There have been some serious developments in our country over the past 13 years, along with the work in waste management. In 2003, the number of regular floor waste storage facilities reached 81, as of the end of 2015. In 2023, it was intended for all citizens to receive regular floor waste storage services. Between 2003-2015, private environmental protection areas were funded by the transfer of $275m to local governments. Again, the wastewater treatment plant was taken to 35 per cent of the municipal population in 2002, while by the end of 2015, it reached 79 per cent. The number of blue flag beaches and marinas, a sign of our clean seas, reached 151 in 2002 and 436 (as of the end of 2015). In order to prevent environmental pollution, administrative sanctions are implemented and preventing environmental pollution, with an average of 37,000 environmental controls per year. IN 13 years, 3.5 billion saplings were met with soil during the AK PARTI rule, while the protected areas were raised from 3.4 million hectares to 5.7 million hectares. Our forest areas have increased by 1.5 million hectares to 22.3 million hectares. The amount of soil carried by erosion was 500 million tons, and the rate was reduced to 168 million tonnes, thanks to erosion control and deforestation. During the AK PARTY administrations, wastewater treatment facilities, drinking water treatment facilities, regular solid waste storage facilities, and natural gas were prevented from air pollution, water pollution, and soil pollution by spreading it throughout all our provinces. On the other hand, it has contributed to the environment by increasing green spaces with landscaping projects, flood control projects, erosion control projects, green belt projects, urban forest projects and recreation projects. With these thoughts and feelings, I wish the 2016 budget to be better, with respect to the General Assembly.

Because, in this process, our basic motto is that man grows in the shadow of man, which is also true in all ancient traditions. One grows in the shadow of a person, especially in the shade of a teacher, because teaching is not only a profession of knowledge but also an art. This art is taught by the masters in perfect terms. Experienced teachers who have spent their years in this profession will be able to introduce to our young friends the subtleties of the profession, the beauty of it, and also some of its challenges. In the presence of counselors, our candidate friends will not only learn the subtleties of the profession, they will also gain a position as a teacher. As the Ministry of National Education, we will be able to make a very important link to the work that took the teacher, the school, the student to the center, and hopefully, with the contribution of all our education workers. It's a very important process for our young teacher candidates because they're going to recognize and learn the principles that guide this community in the process. I'd like to thank our mentors, who have agreed to contribute voluntarily to this important task, once again and wish our candidate teachers well.
